Chains of Enslavement [875] | monster/NPC
    Packet sightings: local 0; other/unassigned 0 (not complete verification)
    Source unresolved; use the linked article/context before planning a trip.
    Historical context/trigger notes:
      This is a spell used by Galen Trask after players accept the Summit Slaves quest. It has the advantage of making all the Stone Summit in Sorrow's Furnace friendly to the party. The disadvantage is that the party cannot attack the Stone Summit. It will stay in effect as long as you don't leave Sorrow's Furnace (even death won't remove it).
      This spell is not technically a hex or enchantment, so skills such as Contemplation of Purity will not remove it.
      This spell has no energy cost, activation time or recharge time.
      The skill itself has no effect, it is there to justify the friendly relations you have with the Stone Summit during the quest.
      Bug.Occasionally a Stone Summit Dwarf will not be allied to you with this effect active. Killing such targets will not affect the allied status of other Stone Summit.
    https://wiki.guildwars.com/wiki/Chains_of_Enslavement

Impending Dhuum [3198] | monster/NPC
    Packet sightings: local 0; other/unassigned 0 (not complete verification)
    Source unresolved; use the linked article/context before planning a trip.
    Historical context/trigger notes:
      Used by Dhuum outside of the Underworld (including outposts and towns).
      Despite what the description states, this skill is also used to block accounts.
      Causes the knock down and burning animations to appear (even in outposts and towns) approximately 1 second before Dhuum appears to block or ban the account; however, this "burning" does not cause health degeneration. In outposts and towns, this skill does not cause the character to lose any health.
      Your character can be resurrected while affected by this skill; however, your account will still be banned.
    https://wiki.guildwars.com/wiki/Impending_Dhuum

Mad King's Fan [2756] | monster/NPC
    Packet sightings: local 0; other/unassigned 0 (not complete verification)
    Source unresolved; use the linked article/context before planning a trip.
    Historical context/trigger notes:
      This hex spell is applied to characters who stand in Mad King Thorn's "glowing circle" in Lion's Arch and Kamadan, Jewel of Istan at the beginning of a Halloween finale (after you follow his first request, which is to kneel). It is removed if you are killed by Mad King Thorn, leave the circle, or rezone.
      If you stay within the glowing circle after the last finale ends, then this effect remains for about 12 hours.
      You receive 1 point towards your Gamer title track for each correct response while still hexed. You receive an additional 100 Trick-or-Treat Bags and 25 Gamer points if this hex is still active on your character at the end of a finale.
      It can be difficult to achieve, since your character can unexpectedly be killed by Mad King Thorn from:
      Being randomly chosen to play rock-paper-scissors and losing.
      Not having each of the four instruments being played by four different players when commanded to.
      This and Scarred Psyche are the only non-PvP methods to get Gamer points.
    https://wiki.guildwars.com/wiki/Mad_King%27s_Fan

Meditation of the Reaper [1276] | monster/NPC
    Packet sightings: local 0; other/unassigned 0 (not complete verification)
    Source unresolved; use the linked article/context before planning a trip.
    Historical context/trigger notes:
      Shared article IDs 1276, 1316; NPC links do not establish which numeric variant is used. This is a monster skill used by Shiro Tagachi when he is at approximately 75% health or below.
      When this skill is used, it is applied to all party members within compass range (i.e. it appears in each party member's effects monitor).
      While this skill is active, Shiro does not attack, move nor use any skills.
      To end this skill early, 500 damage has to be "dealt" to Shiro. While damage is reduced to 0, dealing what would have been 500 will end the skill early.
      As health degeneration, health loss, and life stealing are not considered damage, Shiro can still be harmed by these while this skill is active.
      When this skill ends, Shiro will steal health from all allies within spirit range regardless if they have this skill in their effects monitor (e.g. summoning stone allies or spirits created after this skill was used and is active).
      Bug.On rare occasions, Shiro will remain in his "meditation" even after taking more than 500 damage. He will remain that way for the rest of this mission, but will take damage after the initial 500.
      Anomaly.Similar to Shiro's other skills, the descriptions do not mention that this skill cannot be disabled.
    https://wiki.guildwars.com/wiki/Meditation_of_the_Reaper

Desert Wurm (disguise) [1437] | unclassified non-obtainable; verify access
    Packet sightings: local 0; other/unassigned 0 (not complete verification)
    Source unresolved; use the linked article/context before planning a trip.
    Historical context/trigger notes:
      You enter this disguise by interacting with a Wurm Spoor in the Desolation (provided all members in team have completed Gate of Desolation and that there is no foe nearby).
      While under this disguise, party members are immune to Sulfurous Haze and knock downs.
      The description does not mention that a character's primary attribute is set to 0.
      The Junundu skills will replace all skills of the character inside, except Signets of Capture. The Signets of Capture will still be usable inside the wurm (and the corresponding wurm skill will not be). When capturing a skill while riding a Junundu, the newly captured skill will be replaced by the corresponding Junundu skill. As a result, players cannot use any skills other than Junundu skills while riding a Junundu.
      If you do bring a Signet of Capture avoid covering slot 7, Junundu Wail (your only resurrection skill). Replace Unknown Junundu Ability (if you don't have Junundu Siege yet) or Leave Junundu. Regular skill slot swapping doesn't change which Junundu skill is replaced. When taking a Signet of Capture, enter the Junundu and just look at your skill bar for any skills you wouldn't mind replacing. If so, just leave the Junundu again and adjust your skill arrangement in the Skills and Attributes Panel.
      While under this disguise, party members have 22 less armor rating against elemental damage; they may also have a decreased armor rating against physical damage.
      Only consumables and bonus modifiers (i.e. inscriptions, weapon upgrade components, insignias, runes, and blessings) affect the disguise's maximum health and energy. To apply the bonus modifiers, one must equip or re-equip the armor piece(s) or weapon(s) that decrease(s) (i.e. major and/or superior attribute runes and certain inscriptions) or increase(s) health and/or energy. The maximum health and energy are not affected by the base health and/or energy bonuses on staves, focus items nor armor.
      Upon entering this disguise, certain effects are removed; however, certain consumable effects (i.e. from Armor of Salvation, Essence of Celerity, and Grail of Might) and certain skill effects (i.e. shouts [such as "I Am the Strongest!"], signets [such as Signet of Strength], untyped skills, and weapon spells) will not be removed. You can also keep hold of what you are carrying (i.e. bundle items).
      The disguise has the same energy regeneration of the party member inside. Equipped items with energy regeneration -1 will effect the energy regeneration, such as weapons or offhands with "Seize the Day" or "Live for Today"
      If your weapon has a Vampiric or Zealous modifier on it, you will continue to gain its benefit (Life Draining or 1 energy gain per hit) and drawback (health regeneration -1 or energy regeneration -1).
      While under this disguise, attacks deal a fixed amount of armor-ignoring damage, based on weapon type:
      Axes, hammers, and swords deal 55 damage per hit.
      Daggers deal 40 damage per hit with a chance to double strike.
      Scythes deal 35 damage per hit and can hit two adjacent secondary targets.
      Bows deal 40 damage per hit and have a ranged attack; range varies with bow type.
      Spears deal 55 damage per hit and have a ranged attack.
      Wands and staves deal 40 damage per hit and have a ranged attack.
      Considering the damage listed above and weapon attack speed, but not considering Life Draining if a weapon has a Vampiric modifier:
      Axes and swords will deal more damage per second than hammers.
      Spears will deal more damage per second than hammers. Also, spears have a ranged attack while hammers do not.
      If your weapon's damage has a specific type, the Junundu's weapon is the same. For example, wielding a weapon that deals holy damage causes your Junundu to deal double damage against undead on attacks.
      Morale Boost and Death Penalty does not affect the disguise, but remains on the party member after leaving the Junundu.
      Junundu cannot tunnel through Rocky Ground. Trying to do so will cause the player to leave the Junundu.
      Animal companions die when their owner enters a Junundu, which does not cause Death Penalty. They continue to gain experience normally and will reappear, alive, beside their owner when they leave the Junundu.
      This does not happen during the Gate of Desolation mission. Instead, animal companions will also enter a Junundu. They will have 3,000 health, deal 73 armor-ignoring damage (regardless of type, level, evolution and the owner's Beast Mastery) with attacks and be able to cross sulfur, but will not use skills and will not be resurrected by Junundu Wail. If an animal companion dies in a Junundu, it will reappear as above when its owner leaves their Junundu.
      Since they give constant maximum health and armor rating, Junundu make a great way to power level heroes.
      The Junundu is used for the challenge mission Remains of Sahlahja.
      Junundu models respond to some emote commands:
      /dance : The Junundu wiggles from side to side.
      /sit or /afk : The Junundu burrows in the sand.
      /stand : Just the /sit animation backwards.
      /roar : The Junundu performs the Junundu Wail animation (though not as high), and sometimes with sound.
      Junundu do not show the /kneel emote, but it may be used to summon the Gods' avatars at the resurrection shrines.
      Bug.Sometimes heroes and henchmen will leave their Junundu in the Sulfurous Haze, killing themselves and preventing their resurrection through Junundu Wail.
      Bug.Heroes using a form might not take on the Junundu appearance (the Junundu skill bar will still take place).
      Bug.Although none of the Junundu's skills are spells, using Junundu Feast without an available corpse gives the message "Invalid spell target."
      Disguises
      Obtained through tonics
      Disguised • Avatar of Holiday Cheer • Avatar of Sweetness • Beetle Metamorphosis • Sinister Golem Form • Yeti Form
      Obtained through polymock pieces
      Aloe Seed Form • Bone Dragon Form • Charr Flamecaller Form • Charr Shaman Form • Dolyak Rider Form • Dredge Form • Dwarven Arcanist Form • Earth Elemental Form • Fire Elemental Form • Fire Imp Form • Gaki Form • Gargoyle Form • Ice Elemental Form • Ice Imp Form • Kappa Form • Mantis Dreamweaver Form • Mergoyle Form • Mirage Iboga Form • Mursaat Elementalist Form • Naga Shaman Form • Ruby Djinn Form • Skale Form • Skeletal Mage Form • Smoke Wraith Form • Stone Rain Form • Titan Form • Wind Rider Form
      Obtained during missions
      Corsair • Dastardly • Gwen • Jade Brotherhood • Keiran Thackeray • Kournan Guardsman • Saul D'Alessio • Togo • Turai Ossa
      Obtained in explorable areas
      Desert Wurm • Spirit Form
      Obtained during holiday events
      G.O.L.E.M. • Going Commando • Rollerbeetle Racer • Snowman Form Canthan New Year disguises: Pig Form • Rat Form • Ox Form • Tiger Form • Rabbit Form • Dragon Form • Snake Form • Horse Form • Sheep Form • Monkey Form • Rooster Form • Dog Form Halloween disguises: Agent of the Mad King • Candy Corn Infantry • Combat Costume • Mad King's Influence
      Cannot be obtained by characters
      Lose your Head • Rolling Start • Rudi's Red Nose • Stone Dwarf Transformation • Test Buff
    https://wiki.guildwars.com/wiki/Desert_Wurm_(disguise)

Disarm Trap [1418] | unclassified non-obtainable; verify access
    Packet sightings: local 0; other/unassigned 0 (not complete verification)
    Source unresolved; use the linked article/context before planning a trip.
    Historical context/trigger notes:
      This is a temporary skill that can only be obtained and used during the Venta Cemetery mission. It can be accepted from Rojis at the start of the mission and will remain on the skill bar until completion of the mission.
      Disarm Trap is used to disarm the area of effect damage of the Sentry Traps in the mission.
      Sentry Traps take 5 seconds to activate, so they are easy to disarm as long as one is careful. This skill has an earshot range but does not need a target.
      This skill will take up one slot on your skill bar. In acquiring the skill, you will be asked to choose the skill to replace if your skill bar is full. Make sure to bear this in mind when selecting your build for the mission.
      You can replace Comfort Animal or Heal as One with this skill and still keep control of your pet.
    https://wiki.guildwars.com/wiki/Disarm_Trap

    Historical context/trigger notes:
      Temporarily, during the April Fools' Day quest Annihilator.
      Stats
      While in this disguise, your maximum health is fixed to 600, your maximum energy is fixed to 40, your armor rating is fixed to 80, and you have 4 pips of energy regeneration.
      Equipment properties and consumable effects will not change maximum health, maximum energy or armor rating; they will otherwise work as expected.
      Bug.Contrary to the description, attributes are unaffected.
      Weapon
      Your attacks have melee range, 1.33 second attack speed and hit a single target regardless of weapon equipped.
      Anomaly.While under the effects of this disguise, when zoomed in to first person view, your weapons can be seen floating in the air. This may have been intentional, as it gives the appearance of a first person shooter.

Junundu Tunnel [1442] | unclassified non-obtainable; verify access
    Packet sightings: local 0; other/unassigned 0 (not complete verification)
    Source unresolved; use the linked article/context before planning a trip.
    Historical context/trigger notes:
      This skill is available whenever your character is riding a Junundu, from the Gate of Desolation mission onward through the rest of the Desolation.
      This stance increases average speed by 22% if used every time it recharges.
      If you use Junundu Bite while in this stance, the knock down is applied before the attack is calculated, thus gaining health from Junundu Bite.
      Heroes will only activate this stance while in combat.
      If this stance is active when leaving a Junundu, it will remain in effect for the remaining duration or until you use a skill, and it will still knock down adjacent foes upon ending.
    https://wiki.guildwars.com/wiki/Junundu_Tunnel

Kilroy Stonekin [856] | unclassified non-obtainable; verify access
    Packet sightings: local 0; other/unassigned 0 (not complete verification)
    Source unresolved; use the linked article/context before planning a trip.
    Historical context/trigger notes:
      He appears in the Norn Fighting Tournament as Kilroy (without a last name).
      He appears randomly in certain Far Shiverpeaks explorable areas, where he will offer a repeatable minigame to rampage against the local Stone Summit.
      If you successfully dispatch these foes and keep Kilroy alive, you will receive 30 Dwarf reputation points and a Dwarven Ale; there is no reward if Kilroy dies.
      After accepting the minigame, several groups of Stone Summit will spawn in the surrounding area. Kilroy will charge ahead, typically aggroing many or all of the mobs.
    https://wiki.guildwars.com/wiki/Kilroy_Stonekin

Leave Junundu [1443] | unclassified non-obtainable; verify access
    Packet sightings: local 0; other/unassigned 0 (not complete verification)
    Source unresolved; use the linked article/context before planning a trip.
    Historical context/trigger notes:
      This skill is available whenever your character is riding a Junundu, from the Gate of Desolation mission onward through the rest of the Desolation.
      After using this skill, any Death Penalty you have will begin to affect you again.
      You will not be able to use this skill while in the Sulfurous Haze.
      There are areas in the middle of the Sulfurous Haze where there is solid ground and leaving the Junundu will leave you stranded.
      If you use this skill, all heroes you have in team will also leave their junundu.
      Anomaly.This skill actually takes significantly longer to activate than its listed 0.25¼ second activation time. The progress bar will fill up in the listed quarter second activation time; however, it will flash on the activation bar for several seconds as the Junundu spits out the player. It is not possible to cancel or skip this animation.
    https://wiki.guildwars.com/wiki/Leave_Junundu

STAND UP! [2343] | unclassified non-obtainable; verify access
    Packet sightings: local 0; other/unassigned 0 (not complete verification)
    Source unresolved; use the linked article/context before planning a trip.
    Historical context/trigger notes:
      Passing out counts as a death.
      To avoid passing out, it is possible to go into options and bind STAND UP! to more than one key so you can hit all the keys at the same time and gain energy much more quickly.
      STAND UP! becomes harder as every time you recover, your maximum energy increases by 5.
      You can get no more than 15 energy per second by using this skill.
      Try activating this skill with '8' and clicking on the skill at the same time, to get maximum energy gain.
      Bug.If you gain a skill point whilst knocked out (if Kilroy kills an enemy resulting in you gaining experience), you cannot recover and will pass out.(Does not happen always.)
      Bug.STAND UP! briefly becomes enabled when you kill bosses, even if you are not currently "knocked out". This is due to the game mechanic that "recharges" all skills when you receive a morale boost.
    https://wiki.guildwars.com/wiki/STAND_UP%21

Turai Ossa (disguise) [2652] | unclassified non-obtainable; verify access
    Packet sightings: local 0; other/unassigned 0 (not complete verification)
    Source unresolved; use the linked article/context before planning a trip.
    Historical context/trigger notes:
      You will be in this form during The Battle of Jahai.
      This form provides 2 pips of Energy regeneration.
      You have 84 armor against elemental damage in this form, including Turai's Shield.
      Attributes cannot be increased in this form by using consumables as the inventory will not be available in the respective mission. Adding a "Master of My Domain" to Turai's Shield and a sword pommel of Swordsmanship to Turai's Sword before entering the mission may increase the attribute rank of Healing Signet and Dragon Slash respectively in the mission.
      Excluding "For Elona!" and Giant's Stomp, all of the skills are obtainable warrior skills. However, none of the skills in this form are treated as actual warrior skills. Instead, they are considered common profession skills (including Whirlwind Attack).
      Similarly, despite being available from campaigns, the Dragon Slash and Whirlwind Attack skills in this form are considered Bonus Mission Pack skills rather than Factions and Nightfall skills respectively. Yet the remaining "warrior" skills are considered core skills.
      Your character says "I am Turai Ossa!" when pinging this effect into team chat.
      Disguises
      Obtained through tonics
      Disguised • Avatar of Holiday Cheer • Avatar of Sweetness • Beetle Metamorphosis • Sinister Golem Form • Yeti Form
      Obtained through polymock pieces
      Aloe Seed Form • Bone Dragon Form • Charr Flamecaller Form • Charr Shaman Form • Dolyak Rider Form • Dredge Form • Dwarven Arcanist Form • Earth Elemental Form • Fire Elemental Form • Fire Imp Form • Gaki Form • Gargoyle Form • Ice Elemental Form • Ice Imp Form • Kappa Form • Mantis Dreamweaver Form • Mergoyle Form • Mirage Iboga Form • Mursaat Elementalist Form • Naga Shaman Form • Ruby Djinn Form • Skale Form • Skeletal Mage Form • Smoke Wraith Form • Stone Rain Form • Titan Form • Wind Rider Form
      Obtained during missions
      Corsair • Dastardly • Gwen • Jade Brotherhood • Keiran Thackeray • Kournan Guardsman • Saul D'Alessio • Togo • Turai Ossa
      Obtained in explorable areas
      Desert Wurm • Spirit Form
      Obtained during holiday events
      G.O.L.E.M. • Going Commando • Rollerbeetle Racer • Snowman Form Canthan New Year disguises: Pig Form • Rat Form • Ox Form • Tiger Form • Rabbit Form • Dragon Form • Snake Form • Horse Form • Sheep Form • Monkey Form • Rooster Form • Dog Form Halloween disguises: Agent of the Mad King • Candy Corn Infantry • Combat Costume • Mad King's Influence
      Cannot be obtained by characters
      Lose your Head • Rolling Start • Rudi's Red Nose • Stone Dwarf Transformation • Test Buff

Unknown Junundu Ability [1876] | unclassified non-obtainable; verify access
    Packet sightings: local 0; other/unassigned 0 (not complete verification)
    Source unresolved; use the linked article/context before planning a trip.
    Historical context/trigger notes:
      This skill is available whenever your character is riding a Junundu, from the Gate of Desolation mission until the point where Junundu Siege is available for use.
      This skill shares recharge, activation, and energy cost with Junundu Siege. However, it cannot actually be activated.
      After killing the Elder Siege Wurms as part of the primary quest Horde of Darkness, this skill will be replaced permanently with Junundu Siege.
      This skill will not count toward the Skill Hunter title.
      Mesmer foes in Desolation like Awakened Thought Leech may use their Signet of Humility to disable this skill without any advantage for themselves. It's not clear whether they mistakenly count this skill as harmful or simply use to disable any elite skill on their foes.
    https://wiki.guildwars.com/wiki/Unknown_Junundu_Ability

Vial of Purified Water [1417] | unclassified non-obtainable; verify access
    Packet sightings: local 0; other/unassigned 0 (not complete verification)
    Source unresolved; use the linked article/context before planning a trip.
    Historical context/trigger notes:
      This is a temporary skill that can be obtained and used only during the Nundu Bay mission. It is needed to defeat the Harbingers of Twilight and the Harbinger of Nightfall. It can be obtained by accepting it from Elder Jonah at the start of the mission. It will remain on the skill bar until the mission concludes.
      Anomaly.The Vial of Purified Water is a projectile spell.
      Anomaly.This skill also has the ability to inflict 50 armor-ignoring damage and can interrupt spells, so it can be used against the Margonites in the mission.
      The damage is applied after the vulnerability effect, so the damage does take effect when using it against the Harbingers.
      The damage from this spell is affected by a character's Lightbringer rank.
      As this is a spell, Echo and Arcane Echo can copy it, and Fast Casting decreases its activation time.
    https://wiki.guildwars.com/wiki/Vial_of_Purified_Water
